- **scientific-plot**: Publication-quality scientific figure creation for geoscience research. - **scientific-plots**: Generate matplotlib, seaborn, and Altair figures with colorblind-safe palettes, control detection, and an encoding hierarchy forbidding legends in favour of direct labeling. - **scientific-plotting**: Use when creating publication-quality matplotlib figures in Python — scientific line plots, scatter, error bars, heatmaps, contour, bar, histogram, 3D, multi-panel, log-scale, twin/inset axes, and colorbars. - **scientific-plotting**: 科研绘图助手，用于创建高质量的学术论文图表。支持统计图表（柱状图、折线图、散点图、箱线图、小提琴图）、科学示意图（流程图、机制图）、数据可视化（热力图、网络图）、高级图表（雷达图、Sankey图、分面图）。集成SciencePlots v2.2.1、Plotly交互式可视化、Plotnine等2025-2026最新工具。使用Python (matplotlib/seaborn/scienceplots/plotly/plotnine)、R (ggplot2) 等工具。当用户需要：(1) 绘制论文图表、(2) 数据可视化、(3) 创建科学示意图、(4) 调整图表样式符合期刊要求时触发。关键词：画图、绘图、可视化、plot、figure、chart、科研绘图。 ([Hollis36/claude-skill](https://github.com/Hollis36/claude-skill/tree/HEAD/scientific-plotting) · ★1)
- **scientific-plotting**: Publication-quality plots: vector PDF output, PNG at dpi=300, label and tick font sizes readable at final column width. - **scientific-prose-style**: Prose-quality rules for writing scientific manuscript text: abstract, introduction, results, discussion. The headline rule is a strict em-dash budget: the long dash (—) is overused and reads as a stylistic tic, so cap it and rewrite with commas, colons, parentheses, or a period. Also caps semicolons and colons, the usual replacements, and covers hedging, sentence rhythm, paragraph openers, and AI-sounding prose (去 AI 味, AI 味重, sounds AI-generated) with an information test, a corpus-checked list of tells and a detection script. Puts a concrete subject first, keeps the number next to what it measures and next to its comparison group, puts familiar information before new, and avoids front-loaded modifiers and possessives. This is Skill 3 of the three-skill scientific-writing suite (apply after scientific-results-writing for tone and de-densify-scientific-prose for information load). - **scientific-task-builder**: 对出题者交付的完整 Harbor 候选包实际构建环境、执行 Oracle 参考解及正反例验证，记录日志并报告失败。只负责构建与验证，不修改任务包，不执行正式模型 Solver 或难度校准。 ([wangwenwu2017/sci-skt-web-search-data-pipeline](https://github.com/wangwenwu2017/sci-skt-web-search-data-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-task-builder))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a research question targets architecture or mechanisms (e.g. AgriIR's duplicate-tracking, content-hashing, quality-scoring) that may not be documented in the paper text, so scope must be validated against repos and datasets before execution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/scientific-task-formulation))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a published finding must be turned into a reproducible task: the goal is to re-derive a specific result (e.g. replicate mixing after FastMNN correction without merging resistant-tumor cells) and the source text must be decomposed into datasets, tools, parameters and pass/fail criteria. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scientific-task-formulation))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a section's text must be triaged into research-relevant sub-tasks (e.g. benchmark simulations, ablations) versus setup/maintenance operations, and only bounded scientific tasks with measurable outputs should be emitted. - **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a research question about feature co-association (e.g., CLR scoring of MI networks to discount promiscuous features) must be formulated as a bounded, computable procedure with defined inputs, tools, and outputs before implementation. - **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when you are starting a new mass spectrometry analysis task or feature request where the problem scope is unclear, the tool chain (e.g., OpenMS + Python + KNIME integration) is not yet selected, or acceptance criteria (code quality, test coverage, documentation) have not been established. - **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a research question must be converted into an executable analysis sub-task but the available section text lacks grounded methods steps, so the task scope, required tools, and validation criterion must be defined before analysis can proceed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/scientific-task-formulation))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a candidate sub-task must be scoped to verifiable evidence — e.g. formulating the question of whether HEDGES-MSA overtakes HEDGES only above a specific i.i.d. error rate in the read/write Pareto frontiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scientific-task-formulation--5d0a747f302caefb))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when a source article describes a newly released analysis tool or software package (e.g. RCAT for circadian clock genes) and the agent must decompose the article's claims, delivery artifacts, and workflow into a reusable, executable skill definition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scientific-task-formulation--a05472e003554717))
- **scientific-task-formulation**: Use when you encounter a published scientific article or software paper that makes claims about data processing, analysis, or results, but the reproducibility context is unclear, artifacts are scattered, or the connection between claims and outputs is not immediately evident. ([K-Dense-AI/claude-scientific-writer](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/claude-scientific-writer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/scientific-writing) · ★2.6k · 98 copies)
- **scientific-writing**: Write scientific journal manuscripts in full paragraphs (never bullet points). Use a two-stage process: (1) create section outlines with key points from the literature you have gathered, (2) convert to flowing prose. IMRAD structure, citations (APA/AMA/Vancouver), figures/tables, reporting guidelines (CONSORT/STROBE/PRISMA), for research papers and journal submissions. ([OpenLAIR/dr-claw](https://github.com/OpenLAIR/dr-claw/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-writing) · ★1.2k)
